self = <webpent.production.context_authority.ContextAuthority object at 0x7f7ed6adab10>
principal_ref = PrincipalRef(principal_alias='User_A', engagement_id='e_ca', target_fingerprint='fp_ca', role=<PrincipalRole.FOREIGN_USER: 'foreign-user'>, lifecycle=<ContextLifecycle.USABLE: 'usable'>)
decision_token = {'tool_name': 'httpx_bounded', 'target_url_digest': '037ab55168fe8769f2a4c041c2ea5d8b3112e28b83c9ca8cf7d35445d7a03cbe', 'arguments_digest': 'b8a4120408a76e335316de9a0c139291da653eaffab9cb1406bccf615a0ff495', 'engagement_id': 'e_ca', ...}

    def resolve(self, principal_ref: PrincipalRef, decision_token: dict[str, Any]) -> AuthContextRef:
        """Resolve a principal_ref + decision_token into an AuthContextRef.
    
        Fail-closed: revoked principal, invalid/replayed token, or missing
        vault-bound alias raises and the caller must BLOCK the row.
        """
        if principal_ref.lifecycle != ContextLifecycle.USABLE:
            _audit(self.audit_chain, event_type="context.resolve_denied",
                   principal_alias=principal_ref.principal_alias,
                   engagement_id=self.engagement_id,
                   target_fingerprint=principal_ref.target_fingerprint,
                   error_class="PRINCIPAL_NOT_USABLE",
                   details={"lifecycle": principal_ref.lifecycle.value})
            raise RuntimeError(f"principal {principal_ref.principal_alias!r} is "
                               f"{principal_ref.lifecycle.value}, not usable")
        # Verify decision token (HMAC + single-use nonce). Raises on replay.
        if not verify_decision_token(decision_token):
            _audit(self.audit_chain, event_type="context.resolve_denied",
                   principal_alias=principal_ref.principal_alias,
                   engagement_id=self.engagement_id,
                   target_fingerprint=principal_ref.target_fingerprint,
                   error_class="DECISION_TOKEN_INVALID")
            raise RuntimeError("decision token invalid — fail-closed")
        # Vault-bound alias: confirm credentials exist under the v2 key.
        v2_key = build_v2_vault_key(
            engagement_id=self.engagement_id,
            target_fingerprint=self.target_fingerprint,
            principal_alias=principal_ref.principal_alias,
            role=principal_ref.role.value,
            family="password",
        )
        vault_secret = self._vault_unseal(v2_key)
        if vault_secret is None:
            _audit(self.audit_chain, event_type="context.resolve_denied",
                   principal_alias=principal_ref.principal_alias,
                   engagement_id=self.engagement_id,
                   target_fingerprint=principal_ref.target_fingerprint,
                   error_class="VAULT_ALIAS_NOT_BOUND",
                   details={"vault_key_digest": _digest(v2_key)[:16]})
>           raise RuntimeError(
                f"principal {principal_ref.principal_alias!r} has no vault-bound "
                "credentials under v2 key — x_user_not_vault_bound (B11)"
            )
E           RuntimeError: principal 'User_A' has no vault-bound credentials under v2 key — x_user_not_vault_bound (B11)

sprint_a_target = 'http://127.0.0.1:33871'

    def test_jwt_manipulation_e2e_promoted(sprint_a_target):
        from webpent.vulns.jwt_analyzer import JWTTokenSource
    
        token = encode_jwt({"alg": "none", "typ": "JWT"}, {"sub": "attacker", "role": "admin"}, "")
        variants = {v.scenario: v for v in build_jwt_variants(
            encode_jwt({"alg": "RS256"}, {"sub": "u", "role": "user"}, "sig"),
        )}
        variant = variants["alg_none"]
        hypothesis = variant.as_hypothesis(
            target_url=sprint_a_target, hypothesis_id="h_jwt_e2e",
            protected_path="/api/protected/whoami",
            source=JWTTokenSource(location="header", name="Authorization"),
        )
        # The fixture accepts a tampered alg:none bearer probe; assert our own token
        # shape did not accidentally carry a real signature.
        assert decode_jwt(token)["signature"] == ""
>       _assert_promoted(_run_family(sprint_a_target, "JWT_MANIPULATION", hypothesis))
